# Replay / Production Business-Logic Parity

**Revision:** RPR-0.6-DRAFT  
**Status:** NOT APPROVED Г?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
**Baseline:** `0cb6a9a6cc64ba8ac9d33797bcc30af534fd46e3`; the complete dirty-worktree snapshot must be re-hashed and approved immediately before implementation.  
**Supersedes:** RPR-0.4/RPR-0.5 for review only.

## 1. Business objective and flow

Replay must use current Production paper-trading policies with an isolated
virtual account. It must never read Production account state, call HTTP, or
call candle-only output historical parity.

1. User selects one symbol, virtual profile, window and mode.
2. Qualifier validates every input before a generated Replay artifact exists.
3. A refusal writes only a run/certificate diagnostic.
4. Qualified analysis uses closed, identity-matching candles.
5. Simulation invokes pure Production policies with only virtual adapters.
6. Results stage privately and publish only on terminal success.

Release 1 labels outcomes only `ANALYSIS_PARITY`,
`PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`, or `REFUSED_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`.
`ENTRY_DECISION_PARITY` and historical wallet-lifecycle parity are out of scope:
they require account-state evidence that Replay is forbidden to read.

## 2. Root cause, evidence and scope

Replay currently can include open candles, has null IDs, reads
`wallet_settings`, and differs from Production price/signal management.
Evidence is current source in `RegressionTestWorker`,
`ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ExecutionReplayScope`,
`TradeExecutionValidationService`, `DynamicProfitLockService`,
`LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, and
`PaperTradingService`.

Close-message lineage, deployed JAR/config identity and historical
order-book/derivative/sentiment availability are not durable for all old
windows. Strict claims are therefore unreachable for such eras unless a future
approved forward-capture design is implemented.

In scope: single-symbol virtual simulation; qualifier/refusal; closed-candle
identity; virtual settings/constraints; stable Replay IDs; pure policy ports;
staging; resource limits; tests. Out: Production strategy change, real exchange,
Production account reads, multi-symbol scheduling, and retirement of
FIX-11K/FIX-11Q/pre-FIX122/EXPERIMENTAL. Those paths remain explicit
`EXPERIMENTAL` and cannot enter baseline output.

## 3. Read/write boundary

| Class | Data |
|---|---|
| Forbidden Production account state | `wallet_asset`, `wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, `wallet_settings`, account snapshots and daily account statistics. |
| Permitted read-only evidence | `candle`, `trade_signal`, `market_price_event`, `signal_processing_work`, `candle_analysis_outcome`, `order_book_snapshot`, `fix122_evaluation`, `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, `execution_opportunity`, subject to qualifier as-of rules. |
| Replay-owned writes | `regression_test_run`, `analysis_test_signal`, `wallet_execution_test`, `wallet_position_test`, and new RPR6 profile/certificate/staging objects. |

`NON_CANONICAL` and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` observations are deliberate
exclusions. Required accepted observations are `LEGACY`/`APPLIED`, retaining id
and ordering slot.

## 4. Complete class/file registry

| ID | Artifact | Change | Production impact |
|---|---|---|---|
| RPR6-01 | `RegressionTestWorker` | qualifier before staging; closed identity timeline | none |
| RPR6-02 | `ReplayEvidenceQualifier` new | certificate/mode/refusal | none |
| RPR6-03 | profile/store new | immutable virtual account/config | none |
| RPR6-04 | virtual-account adapter new | balances/positions/effects | none |
| RPR6-05 | `ExecutionReplayScope` | stable run keys, clocks, finally cleanup | shared; no non-Replay behavior change |
| RPR6-06 | `ShadowProductionReplayService` | staging, truthful labels, ordered calls | none |
| RPR6-07 | `TradeExecutionValidationService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
| RPR6-08 | `DynamicProfitLockService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
| RPR6-09 | `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, `PaperTradingService` | expose pure ordered inputs, no policy reorder | shared; transaction trace/regression |
| RPR6-10 | `PositionExitPolicy`, `ProfitLockPolicy`, `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` | virtual constraints, no Replay HTTP | shared; existing Production provider unchanged |
| RPR6-11 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` | experimental profile port and label | Replay only |
| RPR6-12 | `RegimeStateService` | guaranteed Replay cleanup | shared; no Production behavior change |
| RPR6-13 | `ReplayParityValidator` | analysis-only oracle | Replay only |
| RPR6-14 | next verified Flyway migration | profile/certificate/staging/trace schema | additive only |
| RPR6-15 | typed replay configuration | explicit modes and resource bounds | runtime protection |
| RPR6-16 | tests | A8 suite | none |
| RPR6-17 | docs | A10 final docs | none |

## 5. Method contract

| ID | Area | Required behavior |
|---|---|---|
| M01 | worker start | qualifier replaces the later pending-evidence write guard; it runs before any Replay business write. |
| M02 | timeline/snapshot | require `closed=1` and exact symbol/TF/open/close identity; mismatch refuses before staging. |
| M03 | context | select stored as-of evidence, historical recomputation, or refusal per dependency; never fallback to future data. |
| M04 | staging | no skip semantics. Any mid-run invariant/error discards staged artifacts and records only terminal run failure. |
| M05 | profile port | wallet/account/HTTP access in Replay throws named forbidden-dependency failure before publication. |
| M06 | order | price then signal path: advisory stop, TP without extension, label-free lock, rebase/floor, SELL/add suppression, progressive add. Simulation records price assumptions. |
| M07 | virtual budget | virtual daily-check exception ends `FAILED_PROFILE_EVALUATION`; never consults Production account data. |
| M08 | scope | clear Replay and `RegimeStateService` state in `finally` for success, cancel and error. |

## 6. Database/configuration/logging

| ID | Item | Requirement |
|---|---|---|
| D01 | migration | allocate next free migration only after read-only Flyway-history preflight; never edit V103/V106Г?"V109. |
| D02 | MySQL validation | prove baseline/upgrade; V103 collector dependency must be explicit, never silently satisfied. |
| C01 | `replay.default-mode` | `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`. |
| C02 | `replay.max-concurrent-runs` | `1`. |
| C03 | `replay.profile.required` | `true`; no wallet-settings fallback. |
| C04 | executor/pool limit | bounded; reject before harming Production freshness. |
| L01 | INFO `REPLAY_QUALIFIED` | run/mode/baseline/config/evidence counts. |
| L02 | INFO `REPLAY_REFUSED` | run/requested mode/reason codes. |
| L03 | DEBUG `REPLAY_DECISION` | run/key/route/result/evidence IDs/order/clocks. |
| L04 | WARN `REPLAY_FORBIDDEN_DEPENDENCY` | component/dependency type/run, no secret. |
| L05 | INFO `REPLAY_COMPLETED` | earned mode/rows/duration/resources. |

## 7. Trading behavior

Production policy is unchanged. ReplayГ?Ts entry/exit/add/TP/lock/stop behavior
changes only by calling the same extracted pure policies with virtual account
data. Fees/slippage are profile data and zero-fee remains explicitly labelled
when selected. Historical-only/skipped work is reproduced from durable outcome
evidence or analysis parity is refused. Same-boundary higher-timeframe
visibility requires recorded/inferred ordering; otherwise strict analysis claim
is refused.

## 8. Tests and validation

| ID | Scenario | Expected result |
|---|---|---|
| X01 | open/identity-mismatched candle | refusal before output |
| X02 | missing context/config/lineage | analysis claim refused; simulation only with declared inputs |
| X03 | excluded price event status | no false evidence failure |
| X04 | account or HTTP port | fail before publication |
| X05 | Replay signal key | stable and never equal to Production ID; near-TP/consumption works |
| X06 | Production policy fixtures | exact ordered stop/TP/lock/floor/SELL/add outcomes |
| X07 | virtual budget exception | named failed run, no account read |
| X08 | experimental observer | baseline never invokes; output labelled experimental |
| X09 | cancellation/error | state cleanup and staged artifact discard |
| X10 | concurrent Replay/load | one-run/pool bounds preserve freshness |
| X11 | real MySQL migration | next migration, B105/V103 policy and upgrade path proven |
| X12 | baseline hash | CI rejects mismatched source/config request |

## 9. Risks and rollback

Risks: policy drift through shared extraction, latency, look-ahead, Flyway
collision and false labels. Mitigate with pure ports, Production regression and
transaction tests, qualification refusal, preflight and mode metrics. Rollback
disables the new Replay route; it never falls back to wallet reads. Schema is
additive and corrected only forward.

## 10. Documentation and reconciliation

After approved implementation and validation only, update relevant
`codex-docs` and `claude-docs` architecture/changelog/trading/investigation
documents. Shared knowledge/decisions change only through dual agreement.
Implementation report must map every RPR6/M/D/C/L/X item and every actual file
to this registry; any missing or unregistered material work rejects acceptance.

## 11. Approval

Codex: NOT REVIEWED  
Claude: NOT REVIEWED  
Unresolved disagreements: NOT REVIEWED  
User authorization: NOT PROVIDED

No implementation starts until this exact revision is dual-approved, frozen to
the required PDF artifact, and explicitly approved by the user.

# Replay / Production Business-Logic-Parity Redesign

**Revision:** RPR-0.4-DRAFT  
**Status:** DRAFT Г?" NOT APPROVED Г?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
**Created:** 2026-10-10  
**Scope owner:** Codex implementation / Claude independent review

## 1. Business objective

Make manual Replay reproduce the current paper-trading Production business flow
as closely as durable historical evidence permits, while remaining completely
isolated from Production trading state and wallet data.

The exact intended flow is:

```text
historical candle + historical context + canonical accepted price events
  -> shared Production indicator/signal/decision policies
  -> replay-local virtual wallet/account state
  -> shared sizing/allocation/entry revalidation policies
  -> replay-local position lifecycle
  -> shared TP/SL/profit-lock/continuation/exit policies
  -> replay output + explicit parity certificate
```

Replay must never write `wallet_*`, `paper_position`, `trade_signal`,
`signal_processing_work`, or any other Production trading table.

The result is not a claim that historical Java scheduling, MySQL lock timing,
network delay, external Binance availability, or the historical Production
wallet balance can be reproduced. It is a verified **business-logic parity**
claim only when all required market/decision evidence exists. Otherwise Replay
remains available as a labelled simulation and may not claim parity.

## 2. Confirmed root cause and evidence

The 2026-10-10 source audit established the following:

1. Signal, entry, normal-exit, continuation, profit-lock, and near-TP policy
   use several shared Production services.
2. `ShadowProductionReplayService` separately owns replay wallet cash,
   positions, daily budget, persistence, price-loop lifecycle, and result
   labels. It starts at `INITIAL_CAPITAL=10000`, runs one symbol/position, and
   does not reproduce Production wallet/global controls.
3. A run is labelled `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` when at least one accepted price
   event exists, rather than proving coverage for every interval while a
   position was open.
4. Historical derivatives information is not persisted;
   `DerivativesPositioningService.evaluateHistorical()` returns UNAVAILABLE.
   Historical order-book evaluation can likewise be unavailable when snapshots
   were not retained.
5. Replay-only FIX-11K and FIX-11Q observers, and the pre-FIX-122 UI revision,
   have no current Production equivalent and must not contaminate parity runs.
6. Current Replay reads `WalletSettingsRepository` directly and its near-TP
   path can call `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy`, which can request a current
   live Binance minimum notional. Both violate replay account/external-input
   isolation.

## 3. Boundaries

### In scope

- Replay input/evidence qualification and run-level parity status.
- Replay-local virtual wallet state, wallet controls, positions, executions,
  daily statistics, and global limit reproduction.
- Canonical price-event coverage qualification and event ordering.
- Historical context-evidence qualification for derivatives and order book.
- Removal of approved retired Replay-only business/experiment behaviour.
- Parity certificate, diagnostics, migrations, tests, documentation.

### Out of scope

- Any Production strategy change, sizing change, exit change, or wallet change.
- Live-exchange orders, fills, fees, slippage, or order-book execution modelling.
- Replaying database locks, thread scheduling, JVM pauses, WebSocket transport,
  external REST failures, or transaction retries as historical facts.
- FIX-153, FIX-154, and FIX-155 trading-policy changes.
- Deleting Production evidence or existing historical Replay/archive rows.

## 4. Contractual modes

| Mode | Purpose | Missing evidence behaviour | Claim allowed |
|---|---|---|---|
| `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` | Reproduce Production rules using an explicitly selected virtual Replay account and complete market/decision evidence. | Refuse the run before execution. | Business-logic parity. |
| `SIMULATION` | Manual what-if/research run. | Continue but persist all approximation reasons. | Simulation only. |
| `EXPERIMENTAL_RESEARCH` | Explicit, separately approved research such as FIX-155. | Per experiment contract. | Never Production parity. |

The default Administration Replay action must be `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY`; the UI
must make `SIMULATION` and `EXPERIMENTAL_RESEARCH` explicit opt-in choices.

## 5. Business-logic-parity eligibility

A `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` run must fail before generated signals or shadow wallet
state are written unless all of the following hold:

1. Every requested candle identity is closed and within the quality contract.
2. A complete immutable `ReplayAccountProfile` is supplied: starting virtual
   USDT, reserve, daily budget, daily loss state, maximum-new-position/global
   limits, and wallet settings. It is copied to replay-only tables at run start
   and is never read from or written to a Production wallet table.
3. The selected replay universe is declared. The virtual account applies
   global limits across every symbol included in that run; no claim is made
   that it reproduces the historical Production wallet's other positions.
4. The selected profile contains versioned execution constraints for relevant
   symbols, including minimum executable notional. Replay never fetches a
   current exchange constraint while evaluating historical events.
5. Every canonical direct price observation required from the first replay
   position open through its terminal close is represented by accepted
   `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` evidence, with no unresolved `PENDING` row in the whole
   consumed range.
6. Required historical order-book evidence exists whenever the feature was
   enabled for the corresponding Production decision.
7. Required historical derivatives evidence exists whenever derivatives were
   enabled and could change confidence or entry authority. Until durable
   derivatives persistence exists, affected requests must be refused in this
   mode rather than silently treating UNAVAILABLE as equivalent.
8. The exact Production configuration/version fingerprint is available for the
   requested period, or all version-dependent decision paths are marked
   unavailable and the mode is refused.

`SIMULATION` may use configured defaults or fixed starting capital but must
store each deviation in its output. It may never be displayed as parity.

### 5.1 Wallet isolation boundary

Replay never reads Production `wallet_asset`, `wallet_settings`,
`wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, daily-statistics,
or wallet-snapshot rows as a decision input. The existing data does not retain
complete historical wallet state and is deliberately irrelevant to a Replay
account. A user-selected/versioned `ReplayAccountProfile` is the only account
input. This preserves safe isolation while exercising the same pure sizing and
position-management formulas.

The profile also contains symbol execution constraints used by shared
paper-order guards. `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` must be split into a pure
minimum-notional evaluator plus a Production-only exchange-constraint provider;
Replay receives immutable profile values and may not issue Binance HTTP.

## 6. Agreed behaviour changes

### 6.1 Replay starting state

Replace hard-coded starting capital with an immutable `ReplayStartingState`.
It is seeded from the selected, versioned `ReplayAccountProfile` and stored
under the replay run ID before processing begins. Shadow wallet/position
services consume only this replay-local state and replay tables.

The run-start state must include profile ID/version, creation timestamp, version/config fingerprint,
virtual USDT/asset balances, daily-budget state, daily loss state, virtual
managed positions, virtual paper positions, replay idempotency keys, and a
versioned wallet/settings/configuration fingerprint. It may not be refreshed
from Production while a run is executing.

### 6.2 Shared paper-wallet business authority

Extract or complete pure shared policies for:

- initial and progressive sizing;
- reserve, daily budget, daily-loss, maximum-new-position, and global
  maximum-open-position controls;
- buy/sell/partial-harvest quantity and cost-basis calculations;
- position average entry, target/stop updates, profit-lock state, and near-TP
  state transitions;
- idempotency-key interpretation.

Production retains its current transactional repositories. Replay receives
replay-local adapters implementing the same policy inputs/outputs. No policy
may branch on `replay=true` to produce a different business decision.

### 6.3 Price evidence

Replace the boolean/label derived from Г?oany event existsГ?? with a coverage
result containing requested interval, covered interval, gaps, accepted event
count, rejected/unresolved count, and every position-open interval requiring
protection.

`BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` rejects incomplete coverage. `SIMULATION` may use
signal-price fallback only with the persisted reason `PRICE_PATH_INCOMPLETE`.

### 6.4 Historical decision evidence

Persist versioned derivatives and order-book observations required by
Production, or mark affected historical intervals ineligible for business-logic
parity. Replay must use the same normalized evaluator and only evidence at or
before the replay clock. It must never call live Binance endpoints.

### 6.5 Event ordering and time

Use canonical UTC `observed_at` and event IDs for price ordering. Define a
single deterministic tie order matching ProductionГ?Ts committed sequence:

1. accepted price protection event;
2. resulting position/wallet state;
3. closed-candle analysis signal;
4. signal-driven position action.

If durable evidence cannot establish the required tie order, business-logic parity
must refuse the run. Replay timestamps remain presentation-neutral UTC;
KSA conversion belongs only to UI/reporting.

### 6.6 Retired Replay-only behaviour

The following are **candidates for removal**, subject to complete-reference
review and approval. They must be removed from baseline parity runs now; any
future research requires an independently approved module and run mode.

| Registry ID | Candidate | Proposed disposition |
|---|---|---|
| RPR-RM-01 | `DefensiveRiskReductionReplayObserver` / FIX-11K | Remove source, controller/UI/output tables only after archive/export compatibility is proved. |
| RPR-RM-02 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` / FIX-11Q | Remove source, controller/UI/output tables only after archive/export compatibility is proved. |
| RPR-RM-03 | pre-FIX-122 replay revision selector | Remove the UI/API option and old comparison path; Production-current FIX-122 remains the only parity rule. |
| RPR-RM-04 | `EXPERIMENTAL` logic mode if no approved user-facing experiment consumes it | Remove after exact reference search; otherwise retain as an isolated framework with no baseline access. |

`ReplayDataSource.DATABASE` versus `DATASET` is not retired business logic:
both must remain only if a test proves equal outputs. It is a data-access
implementation choice, not an old Production-rule selector.

## 7. Implementation change registry

No implementation item is authorized until this exact revision is approved.

| ID | Area | Required change |
|---|---|---|
| RPR-01 | run contract | Add persisted mode, evidence status, account profile/state ID, version/config fingerprint, and refusal reason to the replay run. |
| RPR-02 | replay account schema/service | Add `ReplayAccountProfile` and immutable run-start-state tables. Seed virtual state only from the selected Replay profile. |
| RPR-03 | wallet authority | Extract/reuse pure paper-wallet policy and provide Production/replay persistence adapters; remove direct Replay reads of `WalletSettingsRepository`. |
| RPR-03a | execution constraints | Split minimum-notional evaluation from live exchange constraint acquisition; inject immutable profile constraints into Replay and prohibit Replay Binance HTTP. |
| RPR-04 | shadow replay | Replace fixed capital/local single-position assumptions with replay-local state and multi-symbol/global-limit coordinator. |
| RPR-05 | price evidence | Add interval coverage validator and replace the current any-event exact-price label. |
| RPR-06 | historical context | Add versioned persistence/lookup/qualification for order book and derivatives, or explicit parity refusal. |
| RPR-07 | timing/order | Persist/use deterministic committed event ordering and reject unknown ordering for parity runs. |
| RPR-08 | certificate | Add immutable parity certificate containing field comparisons, evidence counts, deviations, and final claim. |
| RPR-09 | removals | Execute only the approved RPR-RM entries, including migration/archive compatibility. |
| RPR-10 | UI/API | Make mode explicit; default to business-logic parity; display refusal/deviation/certificate details. |
| RPR-11 | tests | Add unit, integration, MySQL, complete scenario, no-look-ahead, and Production/Replay comparison tests below. |
| RPR-12 | docs | Update architecture, rules, changelog, investigation, operational runbook, and parity contract. |

## 8. Error handling, concurrency, and transactions

- Replay-state creation is isolated and must be consistent for one profile version.
- Business-logic-parity evidence validation happens before replay execution; no
  partial shadow run may be labelled a parity run.
- Replay output writes are transactionally isolated by `test_run_id`; a failed
  run records its failure and certificate status without changing Production.
- Replay must not lock Production wallet/position rows for business mutation.
- Replay-profile/state reads must not expose credentials and must log only IDs,
  UTC times, symbol counts, mode, and evidence result.
- Cancellation preserves isolated run evidence and cannot leave a completed
  parity certificate.

## 9. Logging and observability registry

| ID | Event | Mandatory fields |
|---|---|---|
| RPR-L-01 | virtual starting state created/refused | runId, mode, profile ID/version, virtual balances/position counts, config fingerprint, reason |
| RPR-L-02 | evidence qualified/refused | runId, symbol(s), from/to UTC, candle/price/orderbook/derivatives status, gap counts |
| RPR-L-03 | shadow execution decision | runId, symbol, signal/candle ID, decision code, shared-policy source, replay state ID |
| RPR-L-04 | parity mismatch | runId, field, Production value, replay value, evidence reference, severity |
| RPR-L-05 | certificate completed | runId, claim, matched/mismatched field counts, simulation deviations, duration |
| RPR-L-06 | retired path blocked | runId, removed/experimental feature ID, requested source, outcome |

## 10. Database/configuration/trading impact

- **Database:** additive replay account/snapshot/certificate/evidence schema and any
  required durable historical derivatives observations. Applied migrations are
  never edited. Archive migration/retention is mandatory before deletion of
  retired Replay-only output tables.
- **Configuration:** add replay mode/evidence policy only. Production defaults
  and trading configuration remain unchanged.
- **Trading behaviour:** NONE. Production is read-only to this feature and no
  live/paper Production decision, wallet mutation, position, or protection
  rule may change.
- **Replay behaviour:** baseline becomes evidence-qualified business-logic parity
  or explicitly labelled simulation; retired experimental paths are removed or
  isolated only as approved above.

## 11. Test and validation registry

| ID | Required proof |
|---|---|
| RPR-T-01 | The same profile inputs produce equal Production/replay sizing, reserve, daily budget, daily-loss, and global-position-limit decisions through shared policies. |
| RPR-T-01a | Replay refuses `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` when no immutable replay account profile is selected; it must never silently use a Production wallet row. |
| RPR-T-02 | A missing, unresolved, rejected, or coverage-gapped price event refuses business-logic parity and labels simulation correctly. |
| RPR-T-03 | Price-event/signal same-time ordering matches Production for TP, SL, profit lock, normal exit, and signal SELL. |
| RPR-T-04 | End-to-end initial BUY, progressive add, target extension, rebase, stop, profit lock, near-TP partial harvest, terminal exit, and P&L lifecycle match field-for-field. |
| RPR-T-05 | Historical order book/derivatives use only as-of persisted data; missing evidence refuses parity; no live HTTP occurs. |
| RPR-T-05a | Replay never reads a Production wallet settings row or calls a Binance minimum-notional endpoint; profile values drive the same pure minimum-order decision. |
| RPR-T-06 | Multi-symbol global wallet/max-position test proves no isolated-symbol bypass. |
| RPR-T-07 | Cancellation/error tests prove Production tables remain unchanged and certificates cannot falsely pass. |
| RPR-T-08 | MySQL migration and transaction tests use a disposable database. |
| RPR-T-09 | Dataset and database replay inputs produce equivalent output for the same qualified evidence, or one path is removed. |
| RPR-T-10 | Removal tests prove baseline Replay cannot invoke FIX-11K, FIX-11Q, or pre-FIX-122 paths. |
| RPR-T-11 | Real historical parity suite compares selected known Production windows and produces stored certificates. |

## 12. Risks, rollback, and acceptance

- Risk: historical data required for verified parity may not exist. Mitigation:
  refuse parity and use clearly labelled simulation; do not invent evidence.
- Risk: snapshot schema increases storage. Mitigation: bounded retention and
  archive strategy, never deletion of active/investigation evidence.
- Risk: retiring observers breaks historical UI/archive reads. Mitigation:
  migration and archive/export compatibility tests before removal.
- Rollback: disable new business-logic-parity mode and retain existing archived
  output; Production requires no rollback because it is never mutated.

Acceptance requires: all registry IDs reconciled; no unregistered code change;
all test evidence green; disposable MySQL validation; complete source review by
Claude; Codex verification of the exact reviewed revision; and explicit user
authorization after both agents approve this same document.

   1: # Replay / Production Business-Logic Parity
   2: 
   3: **Revision:** RPR-0.6-DRAFT  
   4: **Status:** NOT APPROVED ƒ?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
   5: **Baseline:** `0cb6a9a6cc64ba8ac9d33797bcc30af534fd46e3`; the complete dirty-worktree snapshot must be re-hashed and approved immediately before implementation.  
   6: **Supersedes:** RPR-0.4/RPR-0.5 for review only.
   7: 
   8: ## 1. Business objective and flow
   9: 
  10: Replay must use current Production paper-trading policies with an isolated
  11: virtual account. It must never read Production account state, call HTTP, or
  12: call candle-only output historical parity.
  13: 
  14: 1. User selects one symbol, virtual profile, window and mode.
  15: 2. Qualifier validates every input before a generated Replay artifact exists.
  16: 3. A refusal writes only a run/certificate diagnostic.
  17: 4. Qualified analysis uses closed, identity-matching candles.
  18: 5. Simulation invokes pure Production policies with only virtual adapters.
  19: 6. Results stage privately and publish only on terminal success.
  20: 
  21: Release 1 labels outcomes only `ANALYSIS_PARITY`,
  22: `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`, or `REFUSED_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`.
  23: `ENTRY_DECISION_PARITY` and historical wallet-lifecycle parity are out of scope:
  24: they require account-state evidence that Replay is forbidden to read.
  25: 
  26: ## 2. Root cause, evidence and scope
  27: 
  28: Replay currently can include open candles, has null IDs, reads
  29: `wallet_settings`, and differs from Production price/signal management.
  30: Evidence is current source in `RegressionTestWorker`,
  31: `ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ExecutionReplayScope`,
  32: `TradeExecutionValidationService`, `DynamicProfitLockService`,
  33: `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, and
  34: `PaperTradingService`.
  35: 
  36: Close-message lineage, deployed JAR/config identity and historical
  37: order-book/derivative/sentiment availability are not durable for all old
  38: windows. Strict claims are therefore unreachable for such eras unless a future
  39: approved forward-capture design is implemented.
  40: 
  41: In scope: single-symbol virtual simulation; qualifier/refusal; closed-candle
  42: identity; virtual settings/constraints; stable Replay IDs; pure policy ports;
  43: staging; resource limits; tests. Out: Production strategy change, real exchange,
  44: Production account reads, multi-symbol scheduling, and retirement of
  45: FIX-11K/FIX-11Q/pre-FIX122/EXPERIMENTAL. Those paths remain explicit
  46: `EXPERIMENTAL` and cannot enter baseline output.
  47: 
  48: ## 3. Read/write boundary
  49: 
  50: | Class | Data |
  51: |---|---|
  52: | Forbidden Production account state | `wallet_asset`, `wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, `wallet_settings`, account snapshots and daily account statistics. |
  53: | Permitted read-only evidence | `candle`, `trade_signal`, `market_price_event`, `signal_processing_work`, `candle_analysis_outcome`, `order_book_snapshot`, `fix122_evaluation`, `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, `execution_opportunity`, subject to qualifier as-of rules. |
  54: | Replay-owned writes | `regression_test_run`, `analysis_test_signal`, `wallet_execution_test`, `wallet_position_test`, and new RPR6 profile/certificate/staging objects. |
  55: 
  56: `NON_CANONICAL` and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` observations are deliberate
  57: exclusions. Required accepted observations are `LEGACY`/`APPLIED`, retaining id
  58: and ordering slot.
  59: 
  60: ## 4. Complete class/file registry
  61: 
  62: | ID | Artifact | Change | Production impact |
  63: |---|---|---|---|
  64: | RPR6-01 | `RegressionTestWorker` | qualifier before staging; closed identity timeline | none |
  65: | RPR6-02 | `ReplayEvidenceQualifier` new | certificate/mode/refusal | none |
  66: | RPR6-03 | profile/store new | immutable virtual account/config | none |
  67: | RPR6-04 | virtual-account adapter new | balances/positions/effects | none |
  68: | RPR6-05 | `ExecutionReplayScope` | stable run keys, clocks, finally cleanup | shared; no non-Replay behavior change |
  69: | RPR6-06 | `ShadowProductionReplayService` | staging, truthful labels, ordered calls | none |
  70: | RPR6-07 | `TradeExecutionValidationService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
  71: | RPR6-08 | `DynamicProfitLockService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
  72: | RPR6-09 | `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, `PaperTradingService` | expose pure ordered inputs, no policy reorder | shared; transaction trace/regression |
  73: | RPR6-10 | `PositionExitPolicy`, `ProfitLockPolicy`, `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` | virtual constraints, no Replay HTTP | shared; existing Production provider unchanged |
  74: | RPR6-11 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` | experimental profile port and label | Replay only |
  75: | RPR6-12 | `RegimeStateService` | guaranteed Replay cleanup | shared; no Production behavior change |
  76: | RPR6-13 | `ReplayParityValidator` | analysis-only oracle | Replay only |

  78: | RPR6-15 | typed replay configuration | explicit modes and resource bounds | runtime protection |
  79: | RPR6-16 | tests | A8 suite | none |
  80: | RPR6-17 | docs | A10 final docs | none |
  81: 
  82: ## 5. Method contract
  83: 
  84: | ID | Area | Required behavior |
  85: |---|---|---|
  86: | M01 | worker start | qualifier replaces the later pending-evidence write guard; it runs before any Replay business write. |
  87: | M02 | timeline/snapshot | require `closed=1` and exact symbol/TF/open/close identity; mismatch refuses before staging. |
  88: | M03 | context | select stored as-of evidence, historical recomputation, or refusal per dependency; never fallback to future data. |
  89: | M04 | staging | no skip semantics. Any mid-run invariant/error discards staged artifacts and records only terminal run failure. |
  90: | M05 | profile port | wallet/account/HTTP access in Replay throws named forbidden-dependency failure before publication. |
  91: | M06 | order | price then signal path: advisory stop, TP without extension, label-free lock, rebase/floor, SELL/add suppression, progressive add. Simulation records price assumptions. |
  92: | M07 | virtual budget | virtual daily-check exception ends `FAILED_PROFILE_EVALUATION`; never consults Production account data. |
  93: | M08 | scope | clear Replay and `RegimeStateService` state in `finally` for success, cancel and error. |
  94: 
  95: ## 6. Database/configuration/logging
  96: 
  97: | ID | Item | Requirement |
  98: |---|---|---|
  99: | D01 | migration | allocate next free migration only after read-only Flyway-history preflight; never edit V103/V106ƒ?"V109. |
 100: | D02 | MySQL validation | prove baseline/upgrade; V103 collector dependency must be explicit, never silently satisfied. |
 101: | C01 | `replay.default-mode` | `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`. |
 102: | C02 | `replay.max-concurrent-runs` | `1`. |
 103: | C03 | `replay.profile.required` | `true`; no wallet-settings fallback. |
 104: | C04 | executor/pool limit | bounded; reject before harming Production freshness. |
 105: | L01 | INFO `REPLAY_QUALIFIED` | run/mode/baseline/config/evidence counts. |
 106: | L02 | INFO `REPLAY_REFUSED` | run/requested mode/reason codes. |
 107: | L03 | DEBUG `REPLAY_DECISION` | run/key/route/result/evidence IDs/order/clocks. |
 108: | L04 | WARN `REPLAY_FORBIDDEN_DEPENDENCY` | component/dependency type/run, no secret. |
 109: | L05 | INFO `REPLAY_COMPLETED` | earned mode/rows/duration/resources. |
 110: 
 111: ## 7. Trading behavior
 112: 
 113: Production policy is unchanged. Replayƒ?Ts entry/exit/add/TP/lock/stop behavior
 114: changes only by calling the same extracted pure policies with virtual account
 115: data. Fees/slippage are profile data and zero-fee remains explicitly labelled
 116: when selected. Historical-only/skipped work is reproduced from durable outcome
 117: evidence or analysis parity is refused. Same-boundary higher-timeframe
 118: visibility requires recorded/inferred ordering; otherwise strict analysis claim
 119: is refused.
 120: 
 121: ## 8. Tests and validation
 122: 
 123: | ID | Scenario | Expected result |
 124: |---|---|---|
 125: | X01 | open/identity-mismatched candle | refusal before output |
 126: | X02 | missing context/config/lineage | analysis claim refused; simulation only with declared inputs |
 127: | X03 | excluded price event status | no false evidence failure |
 128: | X04 | account or HTTP port | fail before publication |
 129: | X05 | Replay signal key | stable and never equal to Production ID; near-TP/consumption works |
 130: | X06 | Production policy fixtures | exact ordered stop/TP/lock/floor/SELL/add outcomes |
 131: | X07 | virtual budget exception | named failed run, no account read |
 132: | X08 | experimental observer | baseline never invokes; output labelled experimental |
 133: | X09 | cancellation/error | state cleanup and staged artifact discard |
 134: | X10 | concurrent Replay/load | one-run/pool bounds preserve freshness |
 135: | X11 | real MySQL migration | next migration, B105/V103 policy and upgrade path proven |
 136: | X12 | baseline hash | CI rejects mismatched source/config request |
 137: 
 138: ## 9. Risks and rollback
 139: 
 140: Risks: policy drift through shared extraction, latency, look-ahead, Flyway
 141: collision and false labels. Mitigate with pure ports, Production regression and
 142: transaction tests, qualification refusal, preflight and mode metrics. Rollback
 143: disables the new Replay route; it never falls back to wallet reads. Schema is
 144: additive and corrected only forward.

 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }

  99:     public int upsert(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 100:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 101:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,

 107:     /** FIX-153: result is valid only after the caller's INPUT transaction commits.
 108:      * Token read-back compares immutable receipt identity, never a racy pre-read of closed state. */
 109:     public Transition upsertRuntime(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 110:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 111:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,

 122:     }
 123:     /** `alreadyClosed` is diagnostic only; the matching token is the sole dispatch authority. */
 124:     public record Transition(boolean transitionedToClosed,Instant closeTime,String token,boolean alreadyClosed) {}

 126:     /**
 127:      * Authoritative recovery is intentionally update-only.  Unlike ordinary
 128:      * REST backfill it can never insert an absent identity and it never makes a
 129:      * runtime close token.  This keeps a late recovery from stealing a normal
 130:      * live close admission.
 131:      */
 132:     public boolean finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 133:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 134:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 135:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
 136:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 137:         return local.update("""
 138:             UPDATE candle SET close_time=?,open_price=?,high_price=?,low_price=?,close_price=?,volume=?,
 139:               quote_asset_volume=?,number_of_trades=?,taker_buy_base_volume=?,taker_buy_quote_volume=?,
 140:               closed=1,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 141:             WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=? AND closed=0
 142:               AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
 143:             """, Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,
 144:             numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime)) == 1;
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     /**
 148:      * Exact authoritative gap repair is pure insert-if-absent.  Do not replace
 149:      * this with ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE: a live row may arrive while REST is in
 150:      * flight and must remain untouched so its normal close can own the token.
 151:      */
 152:     public boolean insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 153:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 154:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 155:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
 156:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 157:         try {
 158:             return local.update("""
 159:                 INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
 160:                     volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
 161:                     closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
 162:                 VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,'BACKFILL',NULL,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
 163:                 """,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
 164:                 closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume) == 1;
 165:         } catch (org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate) {
 166:             return false;
 167:         }

 169:     private int write(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 170:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 171:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 172:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String provenance,String token) {
 173:         String duplicateClause = "BACKFILL".equals(provenance) ? """
 174:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
 175:                 -- REST/backfill is authoritative enough to finish an interrupted open
 176:                 -- candle, but never changes a completed candle, provenance or token.
 177:                 close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
 178:                 open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
 179:                 high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
 180:                 low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
 181:                 close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
 182:                 volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
 183:                 quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
 184:                 number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
 185:                 taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
 186:                 taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
 187:                 updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 188:                 closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
 189:             """ : """
 190:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
 191:                 close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
 192:                 open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
 193:                 high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
 194:                 low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
 195:                 close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
 196:                 volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
 197:                 quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
 198:                 number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
 199:                 taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
 200:                 taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
 201:                 close_transition_token=IF(closed=0 AND VALUES(closed)=1,VALUES(close_transition_token),close_transition_token),
 202:                 updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 203:                 closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
 204:             """;
 205:         // FIX-153: backfill inserts missing identities only, so it cannot fabricate a runtime close.
 206:         return local.update("""
 207:             INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
 208:                 volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
 209:                 closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)

 212:             closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,provenance,token);

   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.config.AnalysisScoringProperties;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
   5: import com.crypto.dto.CandleDataQualityResult;
   6: import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
   7: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   8: import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
   9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  10: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
  11: 
  12: import java.time.Duration;
  13: import java.util.ArrayList;
  14: import java.util.Comparator;
  15: import java.util.List;
  16: 
  17: @Service
  18: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  19: public class CandleDataQualityService {
  20: 
  21:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  22:     private final AnalysisScoringProperties properties;
  23: 
  24:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  25:     public CandleDataQualityResult validate(String symbol, String intervalCode) {
  26:         int minimum = properties.dataQuality().minimumCandles();
  27:         List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(
  28:                 symbol, intervalCode, PageRequest.of(0, minimum)
  29:         ));
  30:         candles.sort(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime));
  31: 
  32:         List<String> warnings = new ArrayList<>();
  33:         int invalid = 0;
  34:         for (Candle candle : candles) {
  35:             boolean invalidOhlc = candle.getOpenPrice() == null
  36:                     || candle.getHighPrice() == null
  37:                     || candle.getLowPrice() == null
  38:                     || candle.getClosePrice() == null
  39:                     || candle.getVolume() == null
  40:                     || candle.getOpenPrice().signum() <= 0
  41:                     || candle.getHighPrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
  42:                     || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
  43:                     || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getHighPrice()) > 0
  44:                     || candle.getVolume().signum() < 0;
  45:             if (invalidOhlc) {
  46:                 invalid++;
  47:             }
  48:         }
  49: 
  50:         Duration expectedStep = intervalDuration(intervalCode);
  51:         int missing = 0;
  52:         if (!expectedStep.isZero()) {
  53:             for (int index = 1; index < candles.size(); index++) {
  54:                 Duration actual = Duration.between(candles.get(index - 1).getOpenTime(), candles.get(index).getOpenTime());
  55:                 if (actual.compareTo(expectedStep) > 0) {
  56:                     long steps = actual.toMillis() / expectedStep.toMillis();
  57:                     missing += Math.max(0, (int) steps - 1);
  58:                 }
  59:             }
  60:         } else {
  61:             warnings.add("Unsupported interval for gap validation: " + intervalCode);
  62:         }
  63: 
  64:         if (candles.size() < minimum) {
  65:             warnings.add("Insufficient history: " + candles.size() + "/" + minimum + " closed candles");
  66:         }
  67:         if (missing > 0) {
  68:             warnings.add("Missing " + missing + " candle(s) in the analysis window");
  69:         }
  70:         if (invalid > 0) {
  71:             warnings.add("Found " + invalid + " candle(s) with invalid OHLCV data");
  72:         }
  73: 
  74:         boolean valid = candles.size() >= minimum
  75:                 && missing <= properties.dataQuality().maximumMissingCandles()
  76:                 && (!properties.dataQuality().blockOnInvalidOhlc() || invalid == 0);
  77: 
  78:         return new CandleDataQualityResult(valid, minimum, candles.size(), missing, invalid, List.copyOf(warnings));
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     private Duration intervalDuration(String intervalCode) {
  82:         if (intervalCode == null || intervalCode.length() < 2) {
  83:             return Duration.ZERO;
  84:         }
  85:         try {
  86:             long amount = Long.parseLong(intervalCode.substring(0, intervalCode.length() - 1));
  87:             char unit = intervalCode.charAt(intervalCode.length() - 1);
  88:             return switch (unit) {
  89:                 case 'm' -> Duration.ofMinutes(amount);
  90:                 case 'h' -> Duration.ofHours(amount);
  91:                 case 'd' -> Duration.ofDays(amount);
  92:                 case 'w' -> Duration.ofDays(amount * 7);
  93:                 default -> Duration.ZERO;
  94:             };
  95:         } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
  96:             return Duration.ZERO;
  97:         }
  98:     }
  99: }

  65:     public List<Map<String, Object>> investigationCases() {

  77:     /**
  78:      * FIX-065: running a saved case delegates to the exact same start() method as the manual form.
  79:      * Therefore the existing single-active-run backend lock and isolated replay tables stay authoritative.
  80:      */
  81:     public synchronized long startInvestigationCase(long caseId) {
  82:         List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
  83:                 SELECT id, case_name, symbol, start_time, end_time
  84:                 FROM regression_investigation_case WHERE id = ?
  85:                 """, caseId);
  86:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Investigation case #" + caseId + " not found.");
  87:         Map<String, Object> row = rows.get(0);
  88:         long runId = start(new RegressionTestRunRequest(
  89:                 String.valueOf(row.get("case_name")), String.valueOf(row.get("symbol")),
  90:                 ((Timestamp) row.get("start_time")).toInstant(), ((Timestamp) row.get("end_time")).toInstant()), ReplayDataSource.DATABASE);
  91:         jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE regression_investigation_case SET last_run_id=? WHERE id=?", runId, caseId);
  92:         return runId;
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     @Transactional
  96:     public synchronized void deleteInvestigationCase(long caseId) {
  97:         int deleted = jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM regression_investigation_case WHERE id=?", caseId);
  98:         if (deleted == 0) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Investigation case #" + caseId + " not found.");
  99:     }

 101:     private void validateInvestigationCase(RegressionInvestigationCaseRequest request) {
 102:         if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
 103:                 || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
 104:                 || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
 105:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Each investigation case requires symbol/start/end and end must be after start.");
 106:         }
 107:     }

 109:     private String blankToNull(String value) {
 110:         return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
 111:     }

 113:     /**
 114:      * FIX-089: only rows that pre-date this service/JVM instance can be leftovers from a real
 115:      * application restart. FIX-088 updated every PENDING/RUNNING row when ApplicationReadyEvent
 116:      * fired, which could falsely mark a replay created by the current JVM as interrupted.
 117:      *
 118:      * Capturing the service construction time makes the distinction deterministic: a replay row
 119:      * created after this instant belongs to the current application instance and must never be
 120:      * touched by startup recovery, even if ApplicationReadyEvent is delivered later than expected.
 160:                     SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped by user',
 161:                         error_message='Replay worker was not active in this application instance; run marked stopped.',
 162:                         completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 163:                     WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')
 164:                     """, runId);
 165:             return Map.of("id", runId, "status", "ERROR", "activeWorker", false,
 166:                     "message", "Test had no active worker and is now stopped. You can Delete Data.");
 167:         }
 168:         jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET current_step='Stop requested ƒ?" waiting for replay worker to exit', heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?", runId);
 169:         return Map.of("id", runId, "status", "STOPPING", "activeWorker", true,
 170:                 "message", "Stop requested. Delete Data will unlock after the replay worker has fully stopped.");
 171:     }

 173:     public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request) {
 174:         return start(request, ReplayDataSource.DATABASE);

 177:     /** FIX-11H explicit replay-only OLD/NEW selector. Production is not configurable through this mode. */
 178:     public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 179:         if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
 180:                 || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
 181:                 || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
 182:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol, start time and end time are required, and end must be after start.");
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
 186:                 SELECT COUNT(*)
 187:                 FROM analysis_test_run
 188:                 WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
 189:                 """, Integer.class);
 190:         if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
 191:             Map<String, Object> active = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap("""
 192:                     SELECT id, test_name, symbol, status, progress_percent, started_at, created_at
 193:                     FROM analysis_test_run
 194:                     WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
 195:                     ORDER BY id DESC
 196:                     LIMIT 1
 197:                     """);
 198:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
 199:                     "Regression test #" + active.get("id") + " is already " + active.get("status")
 200:                             + " for " + active.get("symbol") + ". Wait for it to finish before starting another run.");
 201:         }
 202: 
 203:         String symbol = request.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 204:         ReplayDataSource effectiveSource = dataSource == null ? ReplayDataSource.DATABASE : dataSource;
 205:         String baseTestName = request.testName() == null || request.testName().isBlank()
 330:                 throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
 331:                         "A Replay/Test worker is still active. Use Stop Test and wait until it becomes ERROR before Delete Data.");
 332:             }
 333:             // Defensive cleanup for a stale DB status with no Java worker.
 334:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 335:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped/orphaned worker',
 336:                         error_message='No active Replay/Test worker exists in this application instance.',
 337:                         completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 338:                     WHERE status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')
 339:                     """);
 340:         }
 341: 
 342:         // FIX-088: Delete Data is now a true purge, not an archive operation. Every replay/test
 343:         // table that can feed Recent Test Runs, run detail, Shadow Trades, or archived replay
 344:         // history is emptied. Persistent Proven records are deliberately excluded.
 345:         int provenBefore = countRows("proven_analyzed_trade");
 346:         int provenLegsBefore = countRows("proven_trade_leg_archive");
 347: 
 348:         Map<String, Integer> deleted = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
 349:         // Delete children before parents so the purge remains safe if foreign keys are added later.
 350:         deleted.put("one_candle_continuation_grace_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test"));
 351:         deleted.put("defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test"));
 352:         deleted.put("position_management_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM position_management_test"));
 353:         deleted.put("wallet_execution_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM wallet_execution_test"));
 354:         deleted.put("wallet_position_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM wallet_position_test"));
 355:         deleted.put("execution_opportunity_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM execution_opportunity_test"));
 356:         deleted.put("trade_signal_test", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM trade_signal_test"));
 357:         deleted.put("analysis_test_signal", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_signal"));
 358:         deleted.put("analysis_test_result", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_result"));
 359:         // FIX-122: purge run-owned diagnostics before IDs can be reset; Production audit remains.
 360:         deleted.put("fix122_evaluation", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NOT NULL"));
 361:         deleted.put("fix122_replay_revision", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM fix122_replay_revision"));
 362:         deleted.put("analysis_test_run", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_run"));
 363: 
 364:         deleted.put("one_candle_continuation_grace_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test_archive"));
 365:         deleted.put("defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test_archive"));
 366:         deleted.put("position_management_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM position_management_test_archive"));
 367:         deleted.put("wallet_execution_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM wallet_execution_test_archive"));
 368:         deleted.put("wallet_position_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM wallet_position_test_archive"));
 369:         deleted.put("execution_opportunity_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM execution_opportunity_test_archive"));
 370:         deleted.put("trade_signal_test_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM trade_signal_test_archive"));
 371:         deleted.put("analysis_test_signal_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_signal_archive"));
 372:         deleted.put("analysis_test_result_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_result_archive"));
 373:         deleted.put("analysis_test_run_archive", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM analysis_test_run_archive"));
 374:         deleted.put("regression_test_archive_batch", jdbcTemplate.update("DELETE FROM regression_test_archive_batch"));
 375: 
 376:         // Validate the database state inside this same transaction. If any replay table still
 377:         // contains rows, throw and rollback instead of reporting a false successful purge.
 378:         java.util.List<String> replayTables = java.util.List.of(
 379:                 "one_candle_continuation_grace_test", "defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test", "position_management_test", "wallet_execution_test", "wallet_position_test",
 380:                 "execution_opportunity_test", "trade_signal_test", "analysis_test_signal",
 381:                 "analysis_test_result", "analysis_test_run",
 382:                 "one_candle_continuation_grace_test_archive", "defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test_archive", "position_management_test_archive", "wallet_execution_test_archive",
 383:                 "wallet_position_test_archive", "execution_opportunity_test_archive",
 384:                 "trade_signal_test_archive", "analysis_test_signal_archive",
 385:                 "analysis_test_result_archive", "analysis_test_run_archive",
 386:                 "regression_test_archive_batch");
 387:         Map<String, Integer> remaining = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
 388:         for (String table : replayTables) remaining.put(table, countRows(table));
 389:         java.util.List<String> nonEmpty = remaining.entrySet().stream()
 390:                 .filter(e -> e.getValue() != 0).map(Map.Entry::getKey).toList();
 391:         if (!nonEmpty.isEmpty()) {
 392:             throw new IllegalStateException("Delete Test Data validation failed; rows remain in: " + String.join(", ", nonEmpty));
 393:         }
 394: 
 395:         int provenAfter = countRows("proven_analyzed_trade");
 396:         int provenLegsAfter = countRows("proven_trade_leg_archive");
 397:         if (provenBefore != provenAfter || provenLegsBefore != provenLegsAfter) {
 398:             throw new IllegalStateException("Delete Test Data validation failed; Proven records changed unexpectedly.");
 399:         }
 400: 
 401:         // Reset only tables that own an AUTO_INCREMENT id. This is cosmetic and happens only
 402:         // after successful delete + validation; Proven ids are never reset.
 403:         java.util.List<String> autoIncrementTables = java.util.List.of(
 404:                 "one_candle_continuation_grace_test", "defensive_risk_reduction_observation_test", "wallet_execution_test", "wallet_position_test", "position_management_test",
 405:                 "execution_opportunity_test", "trade_signal_test", "analysis_test_signal",
 406:                 "analysis_test_result", "analysis_test_run", "regression_test_archive_batch");
 407:         for (String table : autoIncrementTables) jdbcTemplate.execute("ALTER TABLE " + table + " AUTO_INCREMENT = 1");
 408: 
 409:         return Map.of(
 410:                 "success", true,
 411:                 "message", "All replay/test data deleted and validated; Proven trades preserved.",
 412:                 "deleted", deleted,
 413:                 "remaining", remaining,
 414:                 "provenTradesPreserved", provenAfter,
 415:                 "provenTradeLegsPreserved", provenLegsAfter
 416:         );
 417:     }
 418: 
 419:     private int countRows(String table) {
 420:         Integer count = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM " + table, Integer.class);
 421:         return count == null ? 0 : count;
 422:     }

 490:     public List<Map<String, Object>> trades(long runId) {

 492:                 SELECT id, test_run_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price, exit_reason,
 493:                        realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent, position_percent, status,
 494:                        EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM proven_analyzed_trade p WHERE p.source_test_run_id = wallet_position_test.test_run_id AND p.source_trade_id = wallet_position_test.id) AS proven_success
 495:                 FROM wallet_position_test
 496:                 WHERE test_run_id = ?

 498:                 LIMIT 500

 505:     /**
 506:      * FIX-11S: one read-only chart payload for an entire Replay run. Replay executions are
 507:      * taken from the isolated wallet_execution_test rows; Production executions are only
 508:      * queried for the same symbol and requested Replay window. An empty Production result
 509:      * is a normal case and is deliberately returned as [] rather than null.
 510:      */
 511:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 512:     public Map<String, Object> allTradesChart(long runId, String interval) {
 513:         List<Map<String, Object>> runs = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 514:                 SELECT id, symbol, start_time, end_time
 515:                 FROM analysis_test_run
 516:                 WHERE id = ?
 517:                 """, runId);
 518:         if (runs.isEmpty()) {
 519:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Replay run not found.");
 520:         }
 521: 
 522:         Map<String, Object> run = runs.get(0);
 523:         String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol")).trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 524:         Timestamp start = (Timestamp) run.get("start_time");
 525:         Timestamp end = (Timestamp) run.get("end_time");
 526:         String normalizedInterval = switch (interval == null ? "5m" : interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
 527:             case "1m", "5m", "1h", "4h" -> interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
 528:             default -> "5m";
 529:         };
 530: 
 531:         List<Map<String, Object>> candles = candleReader(jdbcTemplate).queryForList("""
 532:                 SELECT open_time, open_price, high_price, low_price, close_price, volume
 533:                 FROM candle
 534:                 WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
 535:                 ORDER BY open_time ASC
 536:                 """, symbol, normalizedInterval, start, end);
 537: 
 538:         List<Map<String, Object>> replayExecutions = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 539:                 SELECT id, side, execution_time, execution_price, quantity, notional_usdt,
 540:                        position_percent, signal_interval, signal_decision, execution_source,
 541:                        execution_code, execution_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent
 542:                 FROM wallet_execution_test
 543:                 WHERE test_run_id=? AND symbol=?
 544:                   AND execution_time BETWEEN ? AND ?
 545:                   AND UPPER(side) IN ('BUY','SELL')
 546:                 ORDER BY execution_time ASC, id ASC
 547:                 """, runId, symbol, start, end);
 548: 
 549:         List<Map<String, Object>> productionExecutions = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 550:                 SELECT id, signal_id, side, executed_at AS execution_time, price_usdt AS execution_price,
 551:                        quantity, gross_amount_usdt, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent,
 552:                        execution_type, execution_reason, execution_message
 553:                 FROM wallet_trade
 554:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED'
 555:                   AND executed_at BETWEEN ? AND ?
 556:                   AND UPPER(side) IN ('BUY','SELL')
 557:                 ORDER BY executed_at ASC, id ASC
 558:                 """, symbol, start, end);
 559: 
 560:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 561:         result.put("runId", runId);
 562:         result.put("symbol", symbol);
 563:         result.put("interval", normalizedInterval);
 564:         result.put("from", start);
 565:         result.put("to", end);
 566:         result.put("candles", candles);
 567:         result.put("replayExecutions", replayExecutions);
 568:         result.put("productionExecutions", productionExecutions);
 569:         return result;
 570:     }

 979:     private void persistNearTpPartialHarvest(long runId, String symbol, MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent event,
 980:                                              TradeSignal contextSignal, ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal soldQty,
 981:                                              BigDecimal proceeds, BigDecimal pnl, BigDecimal pnlPct, String explanation) {

 984:             (test_run_id,symbol,side,execution_time,execution_price,quantity,notional_usdt,position_percent,signal_interval,signal_decision,execution_source,execution_code,execution_reason,realized_pnl_usdt,realized_pnl_percent)
 985:             VALUES (?,?,'SELL',?,?,?,?,0,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
 986:             """, runId, symbol, Timestamp.from(event.observedAt()), event.price(), soldQty, proceeds,
 987:                 contextSignal == null ? null : contextSignal.getInterval(),
 988:                 contextSignal == null ? null : name(contextSignal.getDecision()),
 989:                 "LIVE_PRICE_PROTECTION", "NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVEST", explanation, pnl, pnlPct);
 990:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 991:             UPDATE wallet_position_test

 993:                 near_tp_last_1m_signal_id=?, near_tp_harvest_used=1, near_tp_harvested_quantity=?, realized_pnl_usdt=?
 994:             WHERE id=?
 995:             """, p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.nearTpState().name(), p.nearTpBestPrice(), p.nearTpBearishStreak(),

1011:     private String profitLockConfigText() {

   9: import java.math.MathContext;
  10: import java.util.Locale;
  11: 
  12: /**
  13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
  14:  *
  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }

  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69:
