  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
 178: 
 179:     private static final class State {
 180:         final long runId;
 181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
 182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;
 187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
 188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }

  10: /**
  11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
  12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
  16: 
  17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
  18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
  19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
  20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
  21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
  22:         }
  23: 
  24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
  25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
  26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
  27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
  28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
  29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
  30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
  31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
  32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
  33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
  34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
  35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
  36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
  37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
  38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
  42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
  43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: 
  47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
  48: }

  12: /** Approved current-committed input contract. Hashes detect corrected inputs;
  13:  * they do not reconstruct a previous candle version or claim bitemporal Replay. */
  14: public final class CandleInputAudit implements AutoCloseable {
  15:     private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(CandleInputAudit.class);
  16:     private static final ThreadLocal<CandleInputAudit> CURRENT = new ThreadLocal<>();
  17:     private final CandleInputAudit previous;
  18:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  19:     private final String context;
  20:     private final TransactionTemplate writes;
  21:     private int index;
  22:     private CandleInputAudit(JdbcTemplate jdbc, PlatformTransactionManager manager, String context) {
  23:         this.jdbc=jdbc; this.context=context;
  24:         writes=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  25:         // FIX-136: candle validation enters a local read-only JPA transaction.
  26:         // Use the SAME transaction manager to suspend its EntityManager/connection;
  27:         // an inner REQUIRED transaction cannot make that connection writable.
  28:         writes.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  29:         writes.setReadOnly(false);
  30:         writes.setTimeout(5);
  31:         previous=CURRENT.get(); CURRENT.set(this);
  32:     }
  33:     public static CandleInputAudit open(JdbcTemplate jdbc, PlatformTransactionManager manager, String context) {
  34:         return new CandleInputAudit(jdbc,manager,context);
  35:     }
  36:     /** Standalone JDBC callers/tests only. Managed JPA callers must pass their manager. */
  37:     public static CandleInputAudit open(JdbcTemplate jdbc,String context) {
  38:         return open(jdbc,new JdbcTransactionManager(Objects.requireNonNull(jdbc.getDataSource())),context);
  39:     }
  40:     public static void capture(String query,List<Candle> rows) {
  41:         CandleInputAudit audit=CURRENT.get(); if(audit==null)return;
  42:         try {
  43:             var hash=java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
  44:             for(Candle c:rows) hash.update((c.getSymbol()+"|"+c.getIntervalCode()+"|"+c.getOpenTime()+"|"+c.getCloseTime()+"|"+
  45:                 c.getOpenPrice()+"|"+c.getHighPrice()+"|"+c.getLowPrice()+"|"+c.getClosePrice()+"|"+c.getVolume()+"|"+
  46:                 c.getQuoteAssetVolume()+"|"+c.getNumberOfTrades()+"|"+c.getTakerBuyBaseVolume()+"|"+c.getTakerBuyQuoteVolume()+"|"+c.isClosed()+"\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
  47:             audit.writes.executeWithoutResult(tx -> audit.jdbc.update("INSERT INTO shared_candle_input_audit(context_key,read_index,input_hash,row_count,query_text) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)",
  48:                 audit.context,++audit.index,HexFormat.of().formatHex(hash.digest()),rows.size(),query.substring(0,Math.min(1000,query.length()))));
  49:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
  50:             log.error("[FIX-136][AUDIT_WRITE_FAILED] context={}, readIndex={}; analysis must not continue without input evidence",
  51:                     audit.context,audit.index,failure);
  52:             throw failure;
  53:         } catch(java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException e) { throw new IllegalStateException(e); }
  54:     }
  55:     @Override public void close() { if(previous==null) CURRENT.remove(); else CURRENT.set(previous); }
  56: }

  48:     /**
  49:      * FIX-105 / BICOUSDT 26 Aug 2026: FIX-055 intentionally lets the opportunity anchor/best
  50:      * price override the rolling 30-minute low for a fresh opportunity. That protects short-lived
  51:      * setups such as the verified ~71-minute PEPE case from looking artificially cheap merely
  52:      * because the rolling window advanced with price.
  53:      *
  54:      * A multi-hour opportunity must not keep that historical floor forever. BICO replay showed
  55:      * expansion growing monotonically against the opportunity origin and repeatedly producing
  56:      * CHASE_ENTRY_BLOCKED even when fresh 5m confirmation tried to reactivate the setup. Bound the
  57:      * authority of the FIX-055 anchor/best reference to two hours; after that, the exact same shared
  58:      * Production/Replay entry-quality algorithm returns to EVIDENCE_WINDOW's rolling reference.
  59:      * This is not a BUY bypass: ATR extension, reward/risk, opportunity age, rejection-zone risk,
  60:      * volatility penalties and all downstream validation gates remain authoritative.

  15:  *

  17:  * treated as an execution fill. Production resolves the newest canonical Binance 1m
  18:  * live-price event recorded by FIX-052. Administration Replay resolves the newest
  19:  * replay price event already consumed by ExecutionReplayScope. This gives both paths
  20:  * one price authority while preserving the original signal snapshot for audit.</p>
  21:  */
  22: @Service
  23: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  24: public class ExecutionPriceAuthorityService {
  25:     private static final Duration MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE = Duration.ofSeconds(15);
  26: 
  27:     private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
  28:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  29: 
  30:     public Optional<ExecutionPrice> resolve(String symbol, Instant referenceTime) {
  31:         // FIX-109 / Replay parity: wall-clock fallback is forbidden. Every caller must
  32:         // supply the evaluation clock explicitly so historical Replay can never silently
  33:         // compare an old market event with the machine clock used by Production today.
  34:         Instant reference = Objects.requireNonNull(referenceTime,
  35:                 "Execution price reference time is required");
  36: 
  37:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  38:             return replayScope.latestPrice()
  39:                     .filter(p -> symbol != null && symbol.equalsIgnoreCase(p.symbol()))
  40:                     .filter(p -> !p.observedAt().isAfter(reference))
  41:                     .filter(p -> !Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).isNegative())
  42:                     .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
  43:                     .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), "REPLAY_MARKET_PRICE_EVENT"));
  44:         }
  45: 
  46:         return marketPriceEventService.findLatestAtOrBefore(symbol, reference)
  47:                 .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
  48:                 .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), p.source()));
  49:     }

   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.MarketRegime;
   4: import com.crypto.dto.MarketRegimeAssessment;
   5: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: 
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.HashMap;

  14: /**
  15:  * FIX-091 / Fix 4 + FIX-109: Experimental-Replay-only regime persistence state machine.
  16:  * Production behavior is intentionally untouched until the parity gate is passed.
  17:  * State is keyed by replay run + symbol + interval and advances only on candle timestamps.
  18:  */
  19: @Service
  20: public class RegimeStateService {
  21:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  22:     private final int requiredCandles;
  23:     // Run id is part of the key so concurrent Replay/Test workers cannot reset or contaminate
  24:     // one another. The map contains only Replay state; Production never reads it.
  25:     private final Map<String, RegimeState> replayState = new HashMap<>();
  26: 
  27:     public RegimeStateService(ExecutionReplayScope replayScope,
  28:                               @Value("${analysis.regime.persistence-candles:3}") int requiredCandles) {
  29:         this.replayScope = replayScope;
  30:         this.requiredCandles = Math.max(2, requiredCandles);
  31:     }
  32: 
  33:     public synchronized Decision apply(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleTime,
  34:                                        MarketRegimeAssessment detectedAssessment) {
  35:         if (detectedAssessment == null || !replayScope.active() || !replayScope.experimental()) {
  36:             MarketRegime regime = detectedAssessment == null ? MarketRegime.UNKNOWN : detectedAssessment.regime();
  37:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, regime, null, regime, 0, false);
  38:         }
  39: 
  40:         long runId = replayScope.runId();
  41:         String key = runId + "|" + symbol + "|" + interval;
  42:         RegimeState state = replayState.get(key);
  43:         MarketRegime detected = detectedAssessment.regime();
  44:         if (state == null) {
  45:             state = new RegimeState(detected, null, 0, candleTime);
  46:             replayState.put(key, state);
  47:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, detected, null, detected, 0, false);
  48:         }
  49: 
  50:         MarketRegime confirmed = state.confirmed;
  51:         MarketRegime candidate = state.candidate;
  52:         int count = state.candidateCount;
  53:         boolean promoted = false;
  54: 
  55:         if (detected == confirmed) {
  56:             candidate = null;
  57:             count = 0;
  58:         } else if (detected == candidate) {
  59:             count++;
  60:         } else {
  61:             candidate = detected;
  62:             count = 1;
  63:         }
  64: 
  65:         if (candidate != null && count >= requiredCandles) {
  66:             confirmed = candidate;
  67:             candidate = null;
  68:             count = 0;
  69:             promoted = true;
  70:         }
  71:         replayState.put(key, new RegimeState(confirmed, candidate, count, candleTime));
  72: 
  73:         MarketRegimeAssessment effective = detected == confirmed
  74:                 ? detectedAssessment
  75:                 : new MarketRegimeAssessment(confirmed, detectedAssessment.confidence(),
  76:                     append(detectedAssessment.evidence(), "FIX-091 Replay persistence: detected=" + detected
  77:                             + ", candidate=" + candidate + ", confirmed=" + confirmed
  78:                             + ", candidateCount=" + count + "/" + requiredCandles + "."));
  79:         return new Decision(effective, detected, candidate, confirmed, count, promoted);
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     private List<String> append(List<String> evidence, String value) {
  83:         java.util.ArrayList<String> result = new java.util.ArrayList<>(evidence == null ? List.of() : evidence);
  84:         result.add(value);
  85:         return List.copyOf(result);
  86:     }

   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;

   8: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   9: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
  10: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  11: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
  12: 
  13: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  14: import java.math.RoundingMode;
  15: import java.time.Instant;
  16: import java.util.Locale;
  17: 
  18: /**
  19:  * Position-manager profit protection for already-open long positions.
  20:  *
  21:  * The BUY/SELL analysis engine is deliberately not involved here. The lock is
  22:  * derived only from the immutable entry price/take-profit stored on the wallet
  23:  * position and the best price observed after entry.
  24:  *
  25:  * Adaptive policy:
  26:  *  - derive an entry-quality score from the immutable entry signal score and confidence;
  27:  *  - stronger setups receive more room before protection activates;
  28:  *  - weaker/medium setups protect profits earlier;
  29:  *  - the protected price can only move upward and never loosens after activation.
  30:  *
  31:  * Administration values remain the fallback when entry-quality information is unavailable.
  32:  */
  33: @Service
  34: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  35: @Slf4j
  36: public class DynamicProfitLockService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  39:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
  40: 
  41:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository positionRepository;
  42:     private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
  43:     private final ProfitLockPolicy profitLockPolicy;
  44: 
  45:     @Transactional
  46:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal signal) {
  47:         if (signal == null) {
  48:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  49:         }
  50:         return evaluatePrice(signal.getSymbol(), signal.getLatestPrice());
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     /**
  54:      * Evaluates Dynamic Profit Lock directly from a live market price.
  55:      * This path is used by live position protection between candle-close signals.
  56:      *
  57:      * FIX-118 behavioral phase:
  58:      * TP_EXTENSION_REBASE preserves the historically earned lock in persistence but makes
  59:      * it non-executable until progress against the CURRENT TP reaches the configured
  60:      * activation threshold again. This gives an approved continuation meaningful room
  61:      * without forgetting that profit protection had already been earned.
  62:      */
  63:     @Transactional
  64:     public Evaluation evaluatePrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal currentPrice) {
  65:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || currentPrice == null
  66:                 || currentPrice.signum() <= 0) {
  67:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  68:         }
  69: 
  70:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  71:         WalletManagedPosition position = positionRepository
  72:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  73:                 .orElse(null);
  74:         if (position == null) {
  75:             return Evaluation.inactive("No open wallet position exists.");
  76:         }
  77: 
  78:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  79:         if (!settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled()) {
  80:             return Evaluation.inactive("Dynamic Profit Lock is disabled in Administration.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         BigDecimal entry = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt();
  84:         BigDecimal target = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
  85:         BigDecimal current = currentPrice;
  86:         if (entry == null || target == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
  87:             return Evaluation.inactive("The position does not have a valid long take-profit distance.");
  88:         }
  89: 
  90:         ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings);
  91:         BigDecimal activation = profile.activationPercent();
  92:         BigDecimal initialLock = profile.initialLockPercent();
  93:         BigDecimal trailStep = profile.trailStepPercent();
  94:         BigDecimal previousHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
  95:         boolean previousPersistedActive = position.isProfitLockActive();
  96:         BigDecimal previousLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
  97:         BigDecimal previousProgress = position.getProfitLockProgressPercent();
  98:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
  99: 
 100:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest == null ? current : previousHighest.max(current);
 101:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(entry, target, highest);
 102:         BigDecimal lockPrice = previousLock;
 103:         ProfitLockState state = previousState;
 104:         boolean executableActive;
 105:         boolean triggered;
 106: 
 107:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 108:             // Keep the legacy active flag/lock persisted so history is not lost, but do not
 109:             // allow the superseded geometry to trigger an exit during the rebase window.
 110:             executableActive = false;
 111:             triggered = false;
 112:         } else {
 113:             // FIX-118 refinement: when a TP-extension rebase earns activation against the
 114:             // CURRENT target, rebuild the executable lock from the current geometry instead
 115:             // of carrying the historical pre-extension lock through ProfitLockPolicy's
 116:             // monotonic previous-lock rule.
 117:             boolean freshGeometryActivation = previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 118:             ProfitLockPolicy.State policyState = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 119:                     entry, target, current, highest,
 120:                     freshGeometryActivation ? false : (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || previousPersistedActive),

exec

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** RPR-0.6 is not the canonical change contract required by governance. The required artifact remains `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md` at RPR-0.4, while RPR-0.6 explicitly says it supersedes prior drafts “for review only.” It cannot safely receive the required dual-agent approval in its current location/status.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The “complete” registry is materially incomplete and names a nonexistent run table. The implementation uses `analysis_test_run`, not `regression_test_run`; `RegressionTestService` owns run creation, cancellation, purge, archive, reset, and Investigation Queue execution, yet is omitted.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The qualifier-before-write contract is not fully designed. Current `RegressionTestService.start()` creates `analysis_test_run` and `fix122_replay_revision` before the async worker begins. RPR-0.6 registers neither this start path nor the required changes to make a profile/mode available before qualification. The Investigation Queue likewise cannot carry either required value.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** RPR-0.6’s `closed=1` rule does not establish decision-time candle visibility. The current timeline admits open candles; shared snapshot/quality queries use the row’s current closed state. Recovery can subsequently close an existing candle or insert a closed backfill. The current `Candle` projection omits provenance and database timestamps, so it cannot support the proposed historical-finality assertion by itself.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The proposed isolation boundary lacks an enforceable architecture and overlooks an existing forbidden account read. Current Replay directly reads `wallet_settings` and can invoke Binance HTTP for minimum-notional checks. Separately, the current Replay chart endpoint reads `wallet_trade`, which RPR-0.6 absolutely forbids, but the endpoint is not registered as an exemption, removal, or changed display contract.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Price-event ordering cannot meet RPR-0.6 as written. The query orders by `observed_at, id`, but maps to a `PriceEvent` that discards `id`; Replay later sorts price events by timestamp only. `MarketPriceEventService` and the event projection are not in the “complete” registry despite §3 requiring retained ID and ordering slot.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Staging is underspecified. Current Replay persists signals during generation and positions/executions during simulation. RPR-0.6 requires private staging and terminal-only publication but names neither staging objects nor promotion transaction, crash/cancel behavior, archive/purge treatment, retention, or handling of terminal diagnostics. This fails the governance requirement for class/method, database, concurrency, and transaction contracts.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The claimed current-policy scope is incomplete against the current workspace. `ShadowProductionReplayService` invokes `ExecutionIntelligenceService` and `ExecutionPriceAuthorityService`, but neither is registered. Current execution code also contains FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment paths; RPR-0.6 permits associated evidence tables but does not define how those policies, audit availability/failure, and as-of ordering participate in Replay.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `ANALYSIS_PARITY` has no complete earned-claim algorithm. RPR-0.6 acknowledges unavailable deployed JAR/config lineage, but does not define the mandatory fingerprint source, comparison fields, or refusal rule. The existing `ReplayParityValidator` has no production caller and compares only a limited set of signal fields; it is not sufficient evidence for the label.

## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Create one exact, canonical revision in `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`, including a reviewed workspace/source manifest. The currently dirty workspace makes a commit-only baseline insufficient for technical approval.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Add all run-entry, lifecycle, archive, purge, controller/request, UI, and migration artifacts to the registry. Specify profile/mode propagation for both manual and Investigation Queue starts.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Define a single decision-time candle-visibility rule for every primary, BTC, and shared-context read. It must use durable outcome evidence where available, identify clock/tolerance/provenance rules, and refuse strict analysis parity where historical visibility cannot be proven.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Specify an enforceable Replay dependency boundary: concrete interfaces/adapters, async scope propagation, forbidden access behavior, and the disposition of the production-trade overlay.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Define exact staging schemas and atomic promotion, including cancellation, restart, failure, archive, purge, and diagnostic semantics.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Register every actual policy dependency, including execution intelligence/price authority and current FIX-154 paths, or narrow the claim so it does not imply their parity.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Define the precise field/evidence/version/config requirements for each earned label; otherwise remove `ANALYSIS_PARITY` from Release 1.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database, runtime, production system, deployment host, or test suite was accessed or changed.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Historical eligibility rates, deployed configuration/JAR identity, and real-world mismatch rates remain unknown. They cannot validate an earned parity claim until a revised evidence model exists.
